## Review queue

Rows listed here have passing measured automated clauses and need only their manual review clause. Stale decisions remain NOT CHECKED until reviewed against current level/render source.

### global

- **G-11:** Survivor-only weapon pickups have no yellow checkpoint ring; a pickup never respawns (no "unlimited weapons").
- **G-12:** Every following survivor shows its health bar, at every group size (L6/L7 up to 35).
- **G-24:** Health bars: Alphas and infected zoo animals show the world-space bar (damaged or ≤ 15 m); dead ones show none; normal animals none.

### L1

- **L1-02:** 50–60 civilians at start, none idle facing nowhere > 3 s; 0 infected; 0 `combat.*`.
- **L1-03:** Depot clerk scene plays: clerk hands over, walks back into the shop, closes the door; nobody vanishes.
- **L1-04:** Lab handover plays (technician takes the box and walks back in) at PROD quality or better.
- **L1-06:** Exactly 5 infected exit the Annex front door (technician first), ≥ 3 headings ≥ 60° apart.
- **L1-10:** Corgi stiffen/growl/bark before an off-screen infected appears in ≥ 80 % of first encounters.
- **L1-11:** Bicycle: 7.5 m/s, WASD relative to the bike; after dismount it stands on the player's side of any fence/wall.
- **L1-12:** Backyard alleys on the depot/Annex line: no snag on trash bags/pallets/bench corners; the camera-side fence never hides the player.
- **L1-14:** ≥ 6 infected near the garage after the pickup, all from refuge doors (visible ≤ 3 m from the door); at most 3 pursue within 20 m, the rest wait 16 m back.
- **L1-15:** The bay stays open; firefighter repeats "Get in!"; infected stay live until entry; shutter closes with the corgi inside.

### L2

- **L2-01:** 0 infected and 0 `combat.*` until the alarm; alarm at 20–30 s; crew at the truck ≤ 8 s later; 4 civilians on the 2 benches.
- **L2-02:** Axe rack ring visible from the game camera (not behind sign/bollard); axe optional (`no-axe` run finishes).
- **L2-03:** Truck route 15–25 s with player + 6 firefighters seated; all 7 out ≤ 4 s after arrival; control ≤ 0.5 s. Fire-engine crew visible, never hiding the courier.
- **L2-05:** 30 (high) / 20 (low) trapped people visibly panic behind the glass (bang/press/plead loops); first one out ≤ 1.0 s after doorsOpen.
- **L2-06:** Ambush **24** (3 doors × 8, ≥ 3 directions ≥ 60° apart), first visible ≤ 0.5 s after doorsOpen; spread over directions, not one pack.
- **L2-11:** Escape area holds ≥ 25 infected in ≥ 3 groups of ≥ 4, ≤ 20 % chasing at step start; plus **6 scattered singles** along the walk (high; low ≥ 4). PO: "Put a few ones here and there, no group."
- **L2-13:** Panic spreads: a calm ped seeing running peds panics; no ped stays unaffected next to the chaos.
- **L2-14:** Cluster **12–16** (authored 13) on the Elm approach; passable killing ≤ 50 % of it; side path and car alarm each work.
- **L2-15:** Checkpoint: ≥ 3 police vehicles with light bars, ≥ 4 officers firing visibly, ≥ 8 civilians; gate closes ≤ 1.5 s after the player crosses; no infected crosses after.

### L3

- **L3-01:** Handgun only at the armory, within 40 s of start; no reload/ammo UI over 600 shots; 40 HP infected = 2 hits.
- **L3-05:** Camera pulls back ×1.5 while the attack runs and the player is in the yard (east-gate fights in frame).
- **L3-10:** Forward pressure: idle 20 s behind the progress line → 25–75 infected per 10 s from 15–35 m behind, off-screen; stops when the player advances.
- **L3-14:** Army compound: ≥ 8 soldiers, ≥ 2 military vehicles, ≥ 2 buses with civilians; no active infected in the 5 s rest; ring visible during the camera pull-back; evacuation wait **15 s**.
- **L3-15:** Player visible inside the 80-infected horde (silhouette/outline).
